</div><div class="slide" cookie-tracking="ref_page_type=HP;ref_page_sub_type=HP;ref_page_id=%7BD998A0F9-B086-43EE-8377-071A844DD0FC%7D;ref_page_event=Supplier%20Promo%20Click;merch_target=%7B531C88DE-8F16-4274-A9D6-96F8524F80FC%7D;merch_placement=%7Be1e22696-b60f-4cec-9b5a-6f5bd46204e4%7D;merch_id=%7B87e81817-cef7-4143-aff5-687f72ab9d3e%7D" guid="{87e81817-cef7-4143-aff5-687f72ab9d3e}">
<a class="slide-image-link" href="/en/product-highlight/w/wurth-electronics/coaxial-connectors"><img alt="Coaxial Connectors - Wurth" class="img-responsive" src="//www.digikey.ca/-/media/Images/Product%20Highlights/W/Wurth%20Electronics%20Inc/Coaxial%20Cables/Wurth_Coaxial-Connectors_image.jpg?la=en-CA&amp;ts=defbfc89-d432-4d65-8aee-3a91719b323a&amp;h=160&amp;w=240&amp;la=en-CA&amp;hash=E76DDFB6801576DEFD1AC045F0F0F827"/></a> <div class="content">
<h2 class="title">
<a href="/en/product-highlight/w/wurth-electronics/coaxial-connectors">King of Coax Connection</a>
</h2>
<p>The <a href="/en/supplier-centers/w/wurth-electronics" target="_self">Würth Elektronik</a> SMA connector is designed for a frequency range of DC to 18 GHz at 50 Ω impedance. With its threaded-type coupling mechanism, it is perfect for securing your connection in intensive vibration environments.</p>
<p>• Standard interface in accordance with MIL-STD-348 <br/>
• Precise CNC machining with up to 0.003 mm tolerance </p>
<a class="btn primary" href="/en/product-highlight/w/wurth-electronics/coaxial-connectors">Learn More</a>

<h2 class="title">
<a href="/en/product-highlight/i/infineon/xensiv-tle5109">XENSIV™ TLE5109 Magnetic Angle Sensors</a>
</h2>
<p><a href="/en/supplier-centers/i/infineon-technologies" target="_self">Infineon</a>'s AMR-based angle sensors address the need for precise, fast, and cost-efficient angle measurement</p>
<ul>
<li>Available in single- or dual-die versions</li>
<li>Wide magnetic field range: 10 mT up to &gt;500 mT</li>
<li>High angle accuracy with only 0.1° overall angle error (typical)</li>
</ul>
